Persona 4 Golden

100% finite-content completion on the chosen January 2023 Windows release, spoiler-free · 14 areas · 875 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead, it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Open the spoiler-free interactive guide

Route and Save Safety

You are here when: Windows install, title screen, and manual-save menu.

Two protected cycles cover the finite route without exposing story structure.

Chosen Windows Route

Route setup

HintUse the expanded modern release.
WhereUse the expanded modern release; verify it before April scheduling.
Full detailInstall Persona 4 Golden on Windows, confirm the January 2023 feature update is present, and keep its save directory backed up outside the game.

First-Cycle Archive

Route setup

HintBuild statistics and finite town records here.
WhereBuild statistics and finite town records here; verify it before April scheduling.
Full detailUse the first file for every Social Link route you can fit, all quests, books, costumes, equipment, side activities, outcome branches, and a registered pre-clear Compendium.

New Game+ Archive

Route setup

HintOne registry entry and one optional challenge require clear data.
WhereOne registry entry and one optional challenge require clear data; verify it before April scheduling.
Full detailLoad protected clear data, retain the carried ledgers, fuse the New Game+-only Persona, complete the second set of school-club branches, and finish the ultimate optional challenge.

Difficulty Configuration Record

Route setup

HintThe modern port exposes custom parameters.
WhereThe modern port exposes custom parameters; verify it before April scheduling.
Full detailRecord one full clear at the chosen preset, then archive every adjustable difficulty parameter and verify that changing it does not erase finite completion records.

Five-Save Rotation (missable)

Route setup

HintCalendar and outcome gates need separate recovery points.
WhereCalendar and outcome gates need separate recovery points; verify it before April scheduling.
Full detailKeep weekly, monthly, pre-deadline, early-December, and final-day saves. Never overwrite the early-December or final-day branch points.

Carryover Audit

Route setup

HintNot every inventory category survives clear data.
WhereNot every inventory category survives clear data; verify it before April scheduling.
Full detailBefore starting New Game+, record money, social statistics, Compendium, skill-card shop, costumes, max-link keys, and crafted shop stock; separately record levels, equipped Persona, ordinary items, equipment, and analysis as reset categories.

Calendar Control

You are here when: School calendar, town map, bedroom, shrine, and evening streets.

The calendar ledger preserves every finite activity and alternate route.

Matching Arcana

Calendar control

HintCarry a matching Persona before meetings.
WhereCarry a matching Persona before meetings; check it before using the slot.
Full detailHold the contact's Arcana before any rank-building event, confirm the relationship-note bonus, and reload immediately if a required rank fails to advance.

Five Social Statistics

Calendar control

HintRaise every town statistic early.
WhereRaise every town statistic early; check it before using the slot.
Full detailMax Courage, Diligence, Knowledge, Expression, and Understanding through school answers, jobs, books, meals, gardening, fishing, and rainy-day activities.

School-Day Priority

Calendar control

HintSchool contacts lose the most calendar access.
WhereSchool contacts lose the most calendar access; check it before using the slot.
Full detailUse normal school afternoons for campus-only links before town links. Holidays, examinations, and late calendar closures remove those sessions.

Weather Ledger

Calendar control

HintRain changes meetings, enemies, food, and fishing.
WhereRain changes meetings, enemies, food, and fishing; check it before using the slot.
Full detailAudit each rainy day for the special meal, rare enemies, unavailable Social Links, fish conditions, and shop stock before spending the time slot.

Alternate Club Branches (missable)

Calendar control

HintTwo Arcana have mutually exclusive school presentations.
WhereTwo Arcana have mutually exclusive school presentations; check it before using the slot.
Full detailComplete one sports club and one culture club on the first file, then choose the other sport and culture branches on New Game+ and max both alternate Social Links.

Relationship Branches

Calendar control

HintSeveral links offer friendship and romance routes.
WhereSeveral links offer friendship and romance routes; check it before using the slot.
Full detailSave before each commitment, record both available late-link branches locally, then continue from the branch that preserves the calendar and desired archive.

Bike Skill Archive

Calendar control

HintEvery teammate has a finite ride list.
WhereEvery teammate has a finite ride list; check it before using the slot.
Full detailInvite each eligible teammate until every bike-only skill and relearned move has been recorded; keep a pre-ride save so schedule slots can be restored.

Evening Contact Archive

Calendar control

HintNight meetings have finite dialogue and item states.
WhereNight meetings have finite dialogue and item states; check it before using the slot.
Full detailRecord every distinct evening contact, shrine fortune, refrigerator event, and home interaction on branch saves without displacing required rank sessions.

Weekly Completion Audit

Calendar control

HintSmall drift becomes a lost month.
WhereSmall drift becomes a lost month; check it before using the slot.

Town Quests 01-35

You are here when: School corridors, shopping streets, flood plain, riverbank, Okina City, and dungeon return routes.

Accept every numbered request and verify its reward before the calendar closes.

Quest 01, Who's the Riddle Master!?

Town request

HintRecheck school and town request-givers after each calendar change.
WhereFrom April 18th, visit Classroom Building 3F and speak with Funky Student.
Full detailAccept Quest 1 from the listed giver. Choose the first answer, then the second. Confirm 3 Chest Key is received.

Quest 02, The Girl on the Rooftop

Town request

HintRecheck school and town request-givers after each calendar change.
WhereFrom April 25th, visit Class 2-2 and speak with Male Student.
Full detailAccept Quest 2 from the listed giver. Ask the rooftop student about hobbies on three separate visits. Confirm Goho-M is received.

Quest 03, Acquire Mori Ranmaru Shochu

Town request

HintRecheck school and town request-givers after each calendar change.
WhereFrom May 1st, visit Samegawa Flood Plain and speak with Man.
Full detailAccept Quest 3 from the listed giver. Bring Mori Ranmaru to the original requester. Search Former Konishi Liquors (TV World) for the fixed objective item. Confirm 4,000 Yen is received.

Quest 04, Acquire an Angel Statue

Town request

HintRecheck school and town request-givers after each calendar change.
WhereFrom May 2nd, visit Class 2-2 and speak with Timid Female Student.
Full detailAccept Quest 4 from the listed giver. Bring Angel Statue to the original requester. Farm Yukiko's Castle 5-7F and defeat Avenger Knight until the required objective item appears. Confirm 3 Chest Key is received.

The Ramen Way

Book
